<script id="ethvelle-v66-audio-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));
  const AUDIO_DEFAULTS={effect:'none',voice:'natural',pitch:0,enhance:55,enhanceEnabled:false,noiseReduction:false,noiseStrength:65};
  function selectedAudioClip(){
    try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(selectedClip)&&o.type==='audio')||null:null;}catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  window.getEthvelleAudioSettings=function(clip){
    if(!clip)return {...AUDIO_DEFAULTS};
    const cfg=clip.audioSettings=Object.assign({},AUDIO_DEFAULTS,clip.audioSettings||{});
    cfg.pitch=clamp(cfg.pitch,-12,12);
    cfg.enhance=clamp(cfg.enhance,0,100);
    cfg.noiseStrength=clamp(cfg.noiseStrength,0,100);
    if(typeof cfg.enhanceEnabled!=='boolean')cfg.enhanceEnabled=Number(cfg.enhance)>0;
    return cfg;
  };
  window.ethvelleAudioVoiceProfile=function(key){
    const profiles={
      natural:{highpass:30,lowpass:20000,type:'peaking',frequency:1200,q:.55,gain:0},
      clear:{highpass:85,lowpass:17500,type:'highshelf',frequency:3600,q:.7,gain:3.5},
      warm:{highpass:55,lowpass:15000,type:'lowshelf',frequency:210,q:.7,gain:3.2},
      deep:{highpass:48,lowpass:13000,type:'lowshelf',frequency:155,q:.8,gain:4.8},
      bright:{highpass:75,lowpass:19000,type:'highshelf',frequency:4500,q:.7,gain:4.2},
      radio:{highpass:260,lowpass:4400,type:'peaking',frequency:1550,q:.8,gain:4},
      telephone:{highpass:320,lowpass:3400,type:'peaking',frequency:1750,q:.72,gain:3.2},
      soft:{highpass:60,lowpass:11500,type:'highshelf',frequency:5000,q:.7,gain:-3}
    };
    return profiles[String(key||'natural')]||profiles.natural;
  };
  const defs={
    audioEffects:{title:'Audio Effects',property:'effect',items:[['none','Original'],['echo','Echo'],['studio','Studio']]},
    audioVoice:{title:'Voice Filters',property:'voice',items:[['natural','Natural'],['clear','Clear'],['warm','Warm'],['deep','Deep'],['bright','Bright'],['radio','Radio'],['telephone','Telephone'],['soft','Soft']]}
  };
  let activePanel='';
  function sliderCard(id,label,value,min,max,step,unit){
    return `<div class="evca-audio-slider-card"><div class="evca-audio-slider-head"><label for="${id}">${label}</label><output id="${id}Value">${unit==='st'?(Number(value)>0?'+':'')+Number(value).toFixed(1)+' st':Math.round(value)+'%'}</output></div><input class="evca-audio-slider" id="${id}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${value}" oninput="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${id==='evcaAudioPitch'?'pitch':id==='evcaAudioEnhance'?'enhance':'noiseStrength'}',this.value,false);document.getElementById('${id}Value').textContent=${unit==='st'?'(Number(this.value)>0?\'+\':\'\')+Number(this.value).toFixed(1)+\' st\'':"Math.round(Number(this.value))+'%'"}" onchange="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${id==='evcaAudioPitch'?'pitch':id==='evcaAudioEnhance'?'enhance':'noiseStrength'}',this.value,true)"></div>`;
  }
  window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel=function(key){
    const host=$('evcaAudioPanel'),clip=selectedAudioClip();if(!host)return;
    if(!clip){host.innerHTML='';return;}
    activePanel=key||activePanel;
    const cfg=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(clip);
    if(defs[key]){
      const d=defs[key];
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">${d.title}</div><div class="evca-audio-choice-grid">${d.items.map(([v,l])=>`<button type="button" class="evca-audio-choice ${cfg[d.property]===v?'active':''}" onclick="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${d.property}','${v}',true)">${l}</button>`).join('')}</div></div></div>`;
      return;
    }
    if(key==='audioPitch'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel">${sliderCard('evcaAudioPitch','Pitch',cfg.pitch,-12,12,.5,'st')}</div>`;
      return;
    }
    if(key==='audioEnhance'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">Audio Enhancement</div><button type="button" class="evca-audio-toggle ${cfg.enhanceEnabled?'active':''}" onclick="setEthvelleAudioSetting('enhanceEnabled',${!cfg.enhanceEnabled},true)">${cfg.enhanceEnabled?'Enhance On':'Enable Enhance'}</button>${sliderCard('evcaAudioEnhance','Enhance Amount',cfg.enhance,0,100,1,'%')}</div>`;
      return;
    }
    if(key==='audioNoise'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">Background Noise Reduction</div><button type="button" class="evca-audio-toggle ${cfg.noiseReduction?'active':''}" onclick="setEthvelleAudioSetting('noiseReduction',${!cfg.noiseReduction},true)">${cfg.noiseReduction?'Noise Reduction On':'Enable Noise Reduction'}</button>${sliderCard('evcaAudioNoiseStrength','Reduction Strength',cfg.noiseStrength,0,100,1,'%')}</div>`;
    }
  };
  function refreshClipGraph(clip){
    if(typeof renderAudioOverlayMedia!=='function'||!clip)return;
    const now=Number(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0)||0;
    const safeTime=now>=clip.trimStart&&now<clip.trimEnd?now:Math.min(clip.trimEnd-.001,clip.trimStart+.01);
    try{renderAudioOverlayMedia(Math.max(0,safeTime));}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle audio graph refresh failed:',err);}
  }
  window.setEthvelleAudioSetting=function(property,value,commit){
    const clip=selectedAudioClip();if(!clip)return;
    const cfg=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(clip);
    if(property==='pitch')cfg.pitch=clamp(value,-12,12);
    else if(property==='enhance')cfg.enhance=clamp(value,0,100);
    else if(property==='enhanceEnabled')cfg.enhanceEnabled=!!value;
    else if(property==='noiseStrength')cfg.noiseStrength=clamp(value,0,100);
    else if(property==='noiseReduction')cfg.noiseReduction=!!value;
    else if(property==='effect'&&['none','echo','studio'].includes(String(value)))cfg.effect=String(value);
    else if(property==='voice'&&window.ethvelleAudioVoiceProfile(value))cfg.voice=String(value);
    refreshClipGraph(clip);
    if(commit){
      try{persistProjectState();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle audio save:',err);}
      if(activePanel)window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel(activePanel);
      showToast('Audio updated',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1000});
    }
  };

  // Keep audio tools exclusive: ordinary Clip tools do not appear alongside audio controls.
  const priorSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof priorSync==='function'&&!priorSync.__evcaV66){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const row=$('bottomToolsClip');
      // Restore any controls hidden by this wrapper before the original toolbar computes its state.
      if(row)row.querySelectorAll('.tool-item[data-evca-v66-hidden="1"]').forEach(el=>{el.style.display='';delete el.dataset.evcaV66Hidden;});
      const result=priorSync.apply(this,arguments);
      const audio=selectedAudioClip();
      if(row){
        row.classList.toggle('evca-audio-selection',!!audio);
        row.querySelectorAll('.tool-item').forEach(el=>{
          const label=(el.querySelector('span:last-child')?.textContent||'').trim();
          if(audio && (label==='Done'||label==='Delete')) el.classList.add('evca-audio-common-tool');
          else el.classList.remove('evca-audio-common-tool');
          if(audio && !el.classList.contains('evca-audio-only-tool')&&!el.classList.contains('evca-audio-common-tool')){
            el.dataset.evcaV66Hidden='1';el.style.display='none';
          }
        });
        const d=$('bottomToolsDefault'),k=$('bottomToolsKeyframe');
        if(audio){if(d)d.style.display='none';if(k)k.style.display='none';row.style.display='flex';}
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evcaV66=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }

  // Add/repair the live Web Audio chain. The source and final gain are the original nodes
  // created by the project player, so this processing affects the real preview audio.
  const gateLoops=window.__ethvelleAudioGateLoops||(window.__ethvelleAudioGateLoops={});
  function stopGateLoop(id){const entry=gateLoops[id];if(entry&&entry.raf)cancelAnimationFrame(entry.raf);delete gateLoops[id];}
  function setAudioParam(param,value,ctx,time=.025){try{param.setTargetAtTime(value,ctx.currentTime,time);}catch(_e){param.value=value;}}
  function configureLiveGraph(ov,nodes,el,ctx){
    if(!nodes.__evcaV66){
      try{
        const hp=ctx.createBiquadFilter(),voice=ctx.createBiquadFilter(),lp=ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        const analyser=ctx.createAnalyser(),gate=ctx.createGain(),compressor=ctx.createDynamicsCompressor(),presence=ctx.createBiquadFilter(),makeup=ctx.createGain();
        const delay=ctx.createDelay(1),echo=ctx.createGain(),feedback=ctx.createGain();
        analyser.fftSize=1024;analyser.smoothingTimeConstant=.12;
        hp.type='highpass';lp.type='lowpass';presence.type='highshelf';presence.frequency.value=4200;presence.gain.value=1.5;
        // Replace the plain source->volume->speaker route with a processing path feeding the
        // same original volume node; the clip's existing volume slider remains authoritative.
        try{nodes.source.disconnect();nodes.gain.disconnect();}catch(_e){}
        nodes.source.connect(hp);hp.connect(voice);voice.connect(lp);lp.connect(analyser);analyser.connect(gate);gate.connect(compressor);compressor.connect(presence);presence.connect(makeup);makeup.connect(nodes.gain);nodes.gain.connect(ctx.destination);
        gate.connect(delay);delay.connect(echo);echo.connect(nodes.gain);delay.connect(feedback);feedback.connect(delay);
        nodes.__evcaV66={hp,voice,lp,analyser,gate,compressor,presence,makeup,delay,echo,feedback};
      }catch(err){
        console.warn('Ethvelle: audio processing setup failed; restoring direct playback.',err);
        try{nodes.source.disconnect();nodes.gain.disconnect();nodes.source.connect(nodes.gain);nodes.gain.connect(ctx.destination);}catch(_e){}
        return null;
      }
    }
    const cfg=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(ov),profile=window.ethvelleAudioVoiceProfile(cfg.voice),d=nodes.__evcaV66;
    const processed=ov.audioProcessedModes||{},noiseLive=!!cfg.noiseReduction&&!processed.noise,enhanceLive=!!cfg.enhanceEnabled&&!processed.enhance;
    const strength=cfg.noiseStrength/100;
    d.hp.frequency.value=noiseLive?80+strength*100:profile.highpass;
    d.voice.type=profile.type;d.voice.frequency.value=profile.frequency;d.voice.Q.value=profile.q;d.voice.gain.value=profile.gain;
    d.lp.frequency.value=noiseLive?Math.max(6500,14500-strength*4500):profile.lowpass;
    const compression=enhanceLive||cfg.effect==='studio';
    d.compressor.threshold.value=cfg.effect==='studio'?-24:(compression?-25:-6);
    d.compressor.knee.value=cfg.effect==='studio'?18:22;
    d.compressor.ratio.value=cfg.effect==='studio'?5:(compression?3.5:1);
    d.compressor.attack.value=.008;d.compressor.release.value=.18;
    d.presence.gain.value=enhanceLive?(1.2+cfg.enhance/100*2.4):(cfg.effect==='studio'?1.5:0);
    d.makeup.gain.value=enhanceLive?1+cfg.enhance/100*.55:(cfg.effect==='studio'?1.12:1);
    d.delay.delayTime.value=.24;d.echo.gain.value=cfg.effect==='echo'?.22:0;d.feedback.gain.value=cfg.effect==='echo'?.22:0;
    if(!noiseLive){stopGateLoop(ov.id);setAudioParam(d.gate.gain,1,ctx,.02);}
    else if(el&&(!el.paused||el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio==='1')&&!gateLoops[ov.id]){ 
      const data=new Uint8Array(d.analyser.fftSize),entry={raf:0};gateLoops[ov.id]=entry;
      const step=()=>{
        if(gateLoops[ov.id]!==entry)return;
        if(!audioOverlayNodes||audioOverlayNodes[ov.id]!==nodes||!audioOverlayEls||!audioOverlayEls[ov.id]){stopGateLoop(ov.id);return;}
        const liveEl=audioOverlayEls[ov.id];
        if((liveEl.paused&&liveEl.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio!=='1')||!isPlaying){setAudioParam(d.gate.gain,1,ctx,.03);stopGateLoop(ov.id);return;}
        d.analyser.getByteTimeDomainData(data);
        let sum=0;for(let i=0;i<data.length;i++){const v=(data[i]-128)/128;sum+=v*v;}
        const rms=Math.sqrt(sum/data.length),threshold=.0010+strength*.0090;
        const current=d.gate.gain.value;
        const open=current>.5;
        const shouldOpen=open?rms>threshold*.72:rms>threshold*1.2;
        const target=shouldOpen?1:(.015+(1-strength)*.14);
        setAudioParam(d.gate.gain,target,ctx,target<current?.09:.025);
        entry.raf=requestAnimationFrame(step);
      };
      entry.raf=requestAnimationFrame(step);
    } else if(noiseLive && (!el||(el.paused&&el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio!=='1'))) setAudioParam(d.gate.gain,1,ctx,.02);
    const rate=(Number(ov.speed)||1)*Math.pow(2,cfg.pitch/12);
    if(el&&Math.abs(el.playbackRate-rate)>.001)el.playbackRate=rate;
    return d;
  }
  const priorRender=window.renderAudioOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof priorRender==='function'&&!priorRender.__evcaV66){
    const wrapped=function(tSec){
      const result=priorRender.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        Object.keys(gateLoops).forEach(id=>{if(!audioOverlayEls||!audioOverlayEls[id])stopGateLoop(id);});
        (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(o=>o.type==='audio').forEach(ov=>{
          const nodes=audioOverlayNodes&&audioOverlayNodes[ov.id],el=audioOverlayEls&&audioOverlayEls[ov.id];
          if(nodes&&nodes.source&&nodes.gain&&audioOverlayAudioCtx)configureLiveGraph(ov,nodes,el,audioOverlayAudioCtx);
        });
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle live audio processing failed:',err);}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evcaV66=true;window.renderAudioOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }

  // Offline gate for export: short-window RMS with smooth gain transitions, so noise reduction
  // is also baked into the exported audio rather than only affecting editor playback.
  window.ethvelleApplyOfflineNoiseGate=function(buffer,strengthValue){
    try{
      const channels=buffer.numberOfChannels,frames=buffer.length,sr=buffer.sampleRate;
      if(!frames||!channels)return buffer;
      const strength=clamp(strengthValue,0,100)/100,windowSize=Math.max(64,Math.round(sr*.012));
      // Cache each channel once and use a rolling RMS window. This keeps memory overhead
      // tiny on phones (no second full-size AudioBuffer or project-length prefix array).
      const channelData=Array.from({length:channels},(_,c)=>buffer.getChannelData(c));
      const powerWindow=new Float64Array(windowSize);
      const threshold=.0010+strength*.0090,closedGain=.015+(1-strength)*.14;
      let rollingPower=0,gain=1;
      for(let i=0;i<frames;i++){
        let framePower=0;for(let c=0;c<channels;c++){const v=channelData[c][i];framePower+=v*v;}
        framePower/=channels;
        const slot=i%windowSize;
        if(i>=windowSize)rollingPower-=powerWindow[slot];
        powerWindow[slot]=framePower;rollingPower+=framePower;
        const rms=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,rollingPower/Math.min(i+1,windowSize)));
        const target=rms>threshold?1:closedGain;
        gain+=(target-gain)*(target<gain?.025:.09);
        for(let c=0;c<channels;c++)channelData[c][i]*=gain;
      }
      return buffer;
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle offline noise gate skipped:',err);return buffer;}
  };

  // Toolbar synchronization now runs once through the wrapped syncBottomToolsMode call in
  // selectClipForChrome itself. Avoid a second full sync from this selection wrapper.
  const oldDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof oldDeselect==='function'&&!oldDeselect.__evcaV66){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldDeselect.apply(this,arguments);if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();return r;};
    wrapped.__evcaV66=true;window.deselectAllClips=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v67-audio-quality-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));
  const audioClip=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(selectedClip)&&o.type==='audio')||null:null;}catch(_e){return null;}};

  /* Add the Split control to the audio-only toolbar; the original split engine already
     supports overlay clips, and a split copy now receives its own audioSettings object. */
  const split=$('clipSplitTool');if(split)split.classList.add('evca-audio-only-tool');
  const adjust=$('clipAudioAdjustTool');if(adjust)adjust.classList.add('evca-audio-only-tool');

  /* Extend the existing persisted settings without replacing the original getter. */
  const oldGetter=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings;
  if(typeof oldGetter==='function'&&!oldGetter.__evcaV67){
    const wrapped=function(clip){
      const cfg=oldGetter.apply(this,arguments);
      if(clip){
        cfg.pan=clamp(cfg.pan,-100,100);
        cfg.fadeIn=clamp(cfg.fadeIn,0,8);
        cfg.fadeOut=clamp(cfg.fadeOut,0,8);
      }
      return cfg;
    };
    wrapped.__evcaV67=true;window.getEthvelleAudioSettings=wrapped;
  }

  /* One Audio Adjustment panel: pitch, stereo balance, fade-in and fade-out. */
  try{panelContent.audioAdjust={title:'Audio Adjustment',body:'<div id="evcaAudioPanel"></div>'};}catch(_e){}
  const oldPanelRenderer=window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel;
  let activeAudioPanel='';
  function slider(id,label,value,min,max,step,unit,property){
    const formatted=unit==='st'?(Number(value)>0?'+':'')+Number(value).toFixed(1)+' st':unit==='s'?Number(value).toFixed(1)+' s':unit==='pan'?(Number(value)<0?'L '+Math.abs(Math.round(value))+'%':Number(value)>0?'R '+Math.round(value)+'%':'Center'):Math.round(value)+'%';
    const outputExpr=unit==='st'?'(Number(this.value)>0?\'+\':\'\')+Number(this.value).toFixed(1)+\' st\'':unit==='s'?'Number(this.value).toFixed(1)+\' s\'':unit==='pan'?'(Number(this.value)<0?\'L \'+Math.abs(Math.round(Number(this.value)))+\'%\':Number(this.value)>0?\'R \'+Math.round(Number(this.value))+\'%\':\'Center\')':"Math.round(Number(this.value))+'%'";
    return `<div class="evca-audio-slider-card"><div class="evca-audio-slider-head"><label for="${id}">${label}</label><output id="${id}Value">${formatted}</output></div><input class="evca-audio-slider" id="${id}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${value}" oninput="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${property}',this.value,false);document.getElementById('${id}Value').textContent=${outputExpr}" onchange="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${property}',this.value,true)"></div>`;
  }
  function switchField(label,property,checked){
    return `<label class="evca-small-switch"><span>${label}</span><input type="checkbox" ${checked?'checked':''} onchange="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${property}',this.checked,true)"><i></i></label>`;
  }
  function renderAdjustPanel(){
    const host=$('evcaAudioPanel'),clip=audioClip();if(!host)return;
    if(!clip){host.innerHTML='';return;}
    const cfg=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(clip);
    host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">Audio Adjustment</div><div class="evca-audio-adjust-grid">${slider('evcaAudioPitchV67','Pitch',cfg.pitch,-12,12,.5,'st','pitch')}${slider('evcaAudioPanV67','Stereo Balance',cfg.pan,-100,100,1,'pan','pan')}${slider('evcaAudioFadeInV67','Fade In',cfg.fadeIn,0,8,.1,'s','fadeIn')}${slider('evcaAudioFadeOutV67','Fade Out',cfg.fadeOut,0,8,.1,'s','fadeOut')}</div></div>`;
  }
  function renderEnhancePanel(key){
    const host=$('evcaAudioPanel'),clip=audioClip();if(!host)return;
    if(!clip){host.innerHTML='';return;}
    const cfg=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(clip);
    if(key==='audioEnhance'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">Audio Enhancement</div>${switchField('Enable enhancement','enhanceEnabled',!!cfg.enhanceEnabled)}${slider('evcaAudioEnhanceV67','Enhance Amount',cfg.enhance,0,100,1,'%','enhance')}</div>`;
    }else{
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">Background Noise Reduction</div>${switchField('Reduce background noise','noiseReduction',!!cfg.noiseReduction)}${slider('evcaAudioNoiseV67','Reduction Strength',cfg.noiseStrength,0,100,1,'%','noiseStrength')}</div>`;
    }
  }
  window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel=function(key){
    if(key==='audioAdjust'){activeAudioPanel=key;renderAdjustPanel();return;}
    if(key==='audioEnhance'||key==='audioNoise'){activeAudioPanel=key;renderEnhancePanel(key);return;}
    if(typeof oldPanelRenderer==='function')return oldPanelRenderer.apply(this,arguments);
  };
  const oldOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evcaV67){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='audioAdjust'){activeAudioPanel=key;window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel(key);}return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV67=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }

  /* Commit pan/fade properties through the same state and persistence route as other audio controls. */
  const oldSetter=window.setEthvelleAudioSetting;
  window.setEthvelleAudioSetting=function(property,value,commit){
    if(!['pan','fadeIn','fadeOut'].includes(property)){
      const result=oldSetter.apply(this,arguments);
      /* The previous setter can refresh its older panel key; re-render the panel that is open now. */
      if(commit&&activeAudioPanel==='audioAdjust')renderAdjustPanel();
      else if(commit&&(activeAudioPanel==='audioEnhance'||activeAudioPanel==='audioNoise'))renderEnhancePanel(activeAudioPanel);
      return result;
    }
    const clip=audioClip();if(!clip)return;
    const cfg=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(clip);
    cfg[property]=property==='pan'?clamp(value,-100,100):clamp(value,0,8);
    const now=Number(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0)||0;
    const sampleTime=now>=clip.trimStart&&now<clip.trimEnd?now:Math.max(0,clip.trimStart+.01);
    try{if(typeof renderAudioOverlayMedia==='function')renderAudioOverlayMedia(sampleTime);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle audio adjustment preview:',err);}
    if(commit){try{persistProjectState();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle audio adjustment save:',err);}if(activeAudioPanel==='audioAdjust')renderAdjustPanel();showToast('Audio adjustment updated',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1000});}
  };

  /* Apply fades and stereo balance to the existing live DSP output without disturbing volume. */
  const oldAudioRender=window.renderAudioOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldAudioRender==='function'&&!oldAudioRender.__evcaV67){
    const wrapped=function(tSec){
      const result=oldAudioRender.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(o=>o.type==='audio').forEach(clip=>{
          const nodes=audioOverlayNodes&&audioOverlayNodes[clip.id],ctx=audioOverlayAudioCtx,d=nodes&&nodes.__evcaV66;
          if(!nodes||!d||!ctx)return;
          if(!nodes.__evcaV67){
            const fade=ctx.createGain(),pan=(typeof ctx.createStereoPanner==='function')?ctx.createStereoPanner():null;
            try{d.makeup.disconnect();}catch(_e){}
            try{d.echo.disconnect();}catch(_e){}
            if(pan){d.makeup.connect(fade);fade.connect(pan);pan.connect(nodes.gain);d.echo.connect(fade);}
            else{d.makeup.connect(fade);fade.connect(nodes.gain);d.echo.connect(fade);}
            nodes.__evcaV67={fade,pan};
          }
          const cfg=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(clip),extra=nodes.__evcaV67;
          if(extra.pan&&extra.pan.pan)setParam(extra.pan.pan,clamp(cfg.pan,-100,100)/100,ctx);
          const time=Number(tSec)||0,duration=Math.max(.001,clip.trimEnd-clip.trimStart);
          let fi=clamp(cfg.fadeIn,0,8),fo=clamp(cfg.fadeOut,0,8);
          if(fi+fo>duration){const k=duration/(fi+fo);fi*=k;fo*=k;}
          let amount=1;
          if(fi>0)amount=Math.min(amount,clamp((time-clip.trimStart)/fi,0,1));
          if(fo>0)amount=Math.min(amount,clamp((clip.trimEnd-time)/fo,0,1));
          setParam(extra.fade.gain,amount,ctx,.02);
        });
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle live audio adjustment:',err);}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evcaV67=true;window.renderAudioOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }
  function setParam(param,value,ctx,time=.025){try{param.setTargetAtTime(value,ctx.currentTime,time);}catch(_e){param.value=value;}}

  /* Ensure the active-audio toolbar has only audio controls + Split + Done/Delete. */
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evcaV67){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=oldSync.apply(this,arguments),row=$('bottomToolsClip'),clip=audioClip();
      if(row){
        row.classList.toggle('evca-audio-selection',!!clip);
        if(clip){
          const keep=new Set(['clipSplitTool','clipAudioEffectsTool','clipAudioVoiceTool','clipAudioAdjustTool','clipAudioEnhanceTool','clipAudioNoiseTool']);
          row.querySelectorAll('.tool-item').forEach(el=>{
            const label=(el.querySelector('span:last-child')?.textContent||'').trim();
            const common=label==='Done'||(label==='Delete'&&el.id!=='clipDeleteCharacterTool');
            el.classList.toggle('evca-audio-common-tool',common);
            el.classList.toggle('evca-audio-only-tool',keep.has(el.id));
            el.style.display=(keep.has(el.id)||common)?'flex':'none';
          });
          /* V80 owns the parent-row display; this patch only controls audio tool visibility. */
        }else{
          // The base toolbar synchronizer already restored the correct normal clip tools.
          row.classList.remove('evca-audio-selection');
        }
      }
      const adjustTool=$('clipAudioAdjustTool');if(adjustTool)adjustTool.style.display=clip?'flex':'none';
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evcaV67=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }

  /* The base selectClipForChrome now calls the current wrapped syncBottomToolsMode once,
     so a second selection wrapper would repeat the entire toolbar/DOM synchronization.
     Keep the deselect wrapper because deselectAllClips still calls the original lexical
     synchronizer directly and needs the audio-specific toolbar state restored afterward. */
  const oldDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof oldDeselect==='function'&&!oldDeselect.__evcaV67_deselectAllClips){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldDeselect.apply(this,arguments);if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();return r;};
    wrapped.__evcaV67_deselectAllClips=true;window.deselectAllClips=wrapped;
  }

  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();},{once:true});
  else if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v68-final-patch">
/* Ethvelle v68: timeline polish + practical offline audio processing + video AFTER decode repair. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));
  function audioClip(){
    try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&o.type==='audio'&&String(o.id)===String(selectedClip))||null:null;}catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function updateAudioSliderProgress(input){
    if(!input||input.type!=='range')return;
    const min=Number(input.min)||0,max=Number(input.max)||100,value=Number(input.value)||0;
    const pct=max>min?clamp((value-min)/(max-min)*100,0,100):0;
    input.style.setProperty('--evca-progress',pct+'%');
  }
  function wireAudioSliderProgress(host){
    if(!host)return;
    if(!host.dataset.evV68RangeWired){
      host.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.matches('input[type="range"]'))updateAudioSliderProgress(e.target);},true);
      host.addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.matches('input[type="range"]'))updateAudioSliderProgress(e.target);},true);
      host.dataset.evV68RangeWired='1';
    }
    host.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]').forEach(updateAudioSliderProgress);
  }
  function refreshAudioToolbar(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row)return;
    const clip=audioClip();
    if(!clip)return;
    const keep=new Set(['clipSplitTool','clipVolumeTool','clipAudioEffectsTool','clipAudioVoiceTool','clipAudioAdjustTool','clipAudioEnhanceTool','clipAudioNoiseTool']);
    row.querySelectorAll('.tool-item').forEach(el=>{
      const label=(el.querySelector('span:last-child')?.textContent||'').trim();
      const common=label==='Done'||(label==='Delete'&&el.id!=='clipDeleteCharacterTool');
      el.style.display=(keep.has(el.id)||common)?'flex':'none';
      el.classList.toggle('evca-audio-only-tool',keep.has(el.id));
      el.classList.toggle('evca-audio-common-tool',common);
    });
    /* V80 owns the parent-row display; this patch only controls audio tool visibility. */
  }
  const sync0=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof sync0==='function'&&!sync0.__evcaV68){
    const sync=function(){const result=sync0.apply(this,arguments);refreshAudioToolbar();return result;};
    sync.__evcaV68=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=sync;
  }
  ['selectClipForChrome','deselectAllClips'].forEach(name=>{
    const old=window[name];if(typeof old!=='function'||old['__evcaV68_'+name])return;
    const wrapped=function(){return old.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrapped['__evcaV68_'+name]=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });

  /* Avoid repacking every clip lane on every pointer frame. Final pointer-up render still
     rebuilds exact lane positions; during drag only the moved clip position is painted. */
  const oldOverlayLanes=window.applyOverlayLanes;
  if(typeof oldOverlayLanes==='function'&&!oldOverlayLanes.__evcaV68){
    const wrapped=function(groupKey){
      // Live lane reflow is intentionally allowed during a clip drag. It updates only
      // lane top/height styles (no innerHTML rebuild) and keeps overlapping clips separated.
      return oldOverlayLanes.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evcaV68=true;window.applyOverlayLanes=wrapped;
  }
  const oldCharLanes=window.applyCharClipLanes;
  if(typeof oldCharLanes==='function'&&!oldCharLanes.__evcaV68){
    // Character lane packing is now intentionally live during a drag/trim. The base helper
    // only changes lane/row geometry when assignments change, so it stays cheap without
    // postponing the vertical move until pointerup.
    const wrapped=function(){return oldCharLanes.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrapped.__evcaV68=true;window.applyCharClipLanes=wrapped;
  }
  const oldInline=window.setTimelineInlineStyle;
  if(typeof oldInline==='function'&&!oldInline.__evcaV68){
    const wrapped=function(el,property,value){
      const root=$('timelineInner');
      if(el&&property==='left'&&root&&root.classList.contains('clip-drag-active')&&el.classList&&(el.classList.contains('overlay-clip')||el.classList.contains('track-clip'))){
        if(!el.dataset.evV68BaseLeft)el.dataset.evV68BaseLeft=String(parseFloat(el.style.left)||0);
        const base=Number(el.dataset.evV68BaseLeft)||0;
        const wanted=parseFloat(value)||0;
        el.style.transform=`translate3d(${wanted-base}px,0,0)`;
        return;
      }
      return oldInline.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evcaV68=true;window.setTimelineInlineStyle=wrapped;
  }
  function clearDragTransforms(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-ev-v68-base-left],#timelineInner .track-clip[data-ev-v68-base-left],#timelineInner .anim-clip[data-ev-v68-base-left]').forEach(el=>{el.style.transform='';delete el.dataset.evV68BaseLeft;});
  }
  const dragRoot=$('timelineInner');
  if(dragRoot){['pointerup','pointercancel','touchend'].forEach(type=>dragRoot.addEventListener(type,()=>requestAnimationFrame(clearDragTransforms),{passive:true}));}

  /* Actual offline audio renderer. Uses a per-clip spectral noise profile, spectral
     subtraction, a soft dynamics pass for enhancement, then writes PCM WAV audio. */
  let audioDbPromise=null;
  function openAudioDb(){
    if(!('indexedDB' in window))return Promise.reject(new Error('This browser does not support offline audio storage.'));
    if(!audioDbPromise)audioDbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const req=indexedDB.open('ethvelle-audio-processed-v68',1);
      req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{if(!req.result.objectStoreNames.contains('assets'))req.result.createObjectStore('assets');};
      req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not open audio storage.'));
    });
    return audioDbPromise;
  }
  async function saveAudioAsset(key,blob){
    const db=await openAudioDb();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const tx=db.transaction('assets','readwrite');tx.objectStore('assets').put({blob,createdAt:Date.now()},key);
      tx.oncomplete=()=>resolve(key);tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Could not save processed audio.'));
    });
  }
  async function readAudioAsset(key){
    const db=await openAudioDb();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const req=db.transaction('assets','readonly').objectStore('assets').get(key);
      req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result?.blob||null);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not restore processed audio.'));
    });
  }
  function showAudioProgress(title){
    let box=$('evcaV68ProcessOverlay');
    if(!box){box=document.createElement('div');box.id='evcaV68ProcessOverlay';box.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-v68-progress-card"><div class="evca-v68-progress-title" id="evcaV68ProgressTitle"></div><div class="evca-v68-progress-sub" id="evcaV68ProgressSub">Preparing audio…</div><div class="evca-v68-progress-track"><i id="evcaV68ProgressFill"></i></div><div class="evca-v68-progress-number" id="evcaV68ProgressNumber">0%</div></div>`;document.body.appendChild(box);}
    $('evcaV68ProgressTitle').textContent=title;$('evcaV68ProgressSub').textContent='Preparing audio…';$('evcaV68ProgressFill').style.width='0%';$('evcaV68ProgressNumber').textContent='0%';box.classList.remove('evca-applied');box.classList.add('active');
    return (pct,msg,done=false)=>{const p=clamp(pct,0,100);$('evcaV68ProgressFill').style.width=p+'%';$('evcaV68ProgressNumber').textContent=Math.round(p)+'%';$('evcaV68ProgressSub').textContent=done?'Applied':(msg||'');if(done){box.classList.add('evca-applied');$('evcaV68ProgressTitle').textContent='Applied';}};
  }
  const yieldUI=()=>new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
  function fft(re,im,inverse){
    const n=re.length;
    for(let i=1,j=0;i<n;i++){
      let bit=n>>1;for(;j&bit;bit>>=1)j^=bit;j^=bit;
      if(i<j){let t=re[i];re[i]=re[j];re[j]=t;t=im[i];im[i]=im[j];im[j]=t;}
    }
    for(let len=2;len<=n;len<<=1){
      const angle=(inverse?2:-2)*Math.PI/len,wr0=Math.cos(angle),wi0=Math.sin(angle);
      for(let i=0;i<n;i+=len){let wr=1,wi=0;const half=len>>1;
        for(let j=0;j<half;j++){
          const u=i+j,v=u+half,vr=re[v]*wr-im[v]*wi,vi=re[v]*wi+im[v]*wr;
          re[v]=re[u]-vr;im[v]=im[u]-vi;re[u]+=vr;im[u]+=vi;
          const nwr=wr*wr0-wi*wi0;wi=wr*wi0+wi*wr0;wr=nwr;
        }
      }
    }
    if(inverse)for(let i=0;i<n;i++){re[i]/=n;im[i]/=n;}
  }
  async function spectralClean(buffer,mode,strength,onProgress){
    const fftSize=1024,hop=256,half=fftSize>>1,windowFn=new Float32Array(fftSize);
    for(let i=0;i<fftSize;i++)windowFn[i]=Math.sqrt(.5-.5*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*i/(fftSize-1)));
    const channels=[],totalFrames=Math.ceil(buffer.length/hop),channelCount=buffer.numberOfChannels;
    for(let ch=0;ch<channelCount;ch++){
      const source=buffer.getChannelData(ch),out=new Float32Array(buffer.length),weight=new Float32Array(buffer.length);
      const starts=[];for(let s=-fftSize+hop;s<buffer.length;s+=hop)starts.push(s);
      const training=[];for(const s of starts){if(s>buffer.sampleRate*1.8)break;let sum=0,n=0;for(let i=0;i<fftSize;i+=4){const ix=s+i;if(ix>=0&&ix<source.length){const v=source[ix];sum+=v*v;n++;}}if(n)training.push({s,rms:Math.sqrt(sum/n)});}
      training.sort((a,b)=>a.rms-b.rms);const quiet=training.slice(0,Math.max(3,Math.ceil(training.length*.24)));
      const profile=new Float32Array(half+1),re=new Float64Array(fftSize),im=new Float64Array(fftSize);
      for(const item of quiet){re.fill(0);im.fill(0);for(let i=0;i<fftSize;i++){const ix=item.s+i;re[i]=(ix>=0&&ix<source.length?source[ix]:0)*windowFn[i];}fft(re,im,false);for(let k=0;k<=half;k++)profile[k]+=Math.hypot(re[k],im[k]);}
      const denom=Math.max(1,quiet.length);for(let k=0;k<=half;k++)profile[k]/=denom;
      // A short moving average keeps the learned noise floor smooth between FFT bins.
      const smooth=profile.slice();for(let k=1;k<half;k++)profile[k]=(smooth[k-1]+2*smooth[k]+smooth[k+1])/4;
      const modeFactor=mode==='noise'?(0.95+strength*1.8):(0.25+strength*.62);
      const floorGain=mode==='noise'?(0.035+(1-strength)*.20):.42;
      let done=0;
      for(const s of starts){
        re.fill(0);im.fill(0);
        for(let i=0;i<fftSize;i++){const ix=s+i;re[i]=(ix>=0&&ix<source.length?source[ix]:0)*windowFn[i];}
        fft(re,im,false);
        const gains=new Float32Array(half+1);
        for(let k=0;k<=half;k++){
          const mag=Math.hypot(re[k],im[k]),noise=profile[k]*modeFactor;
          const raw=mag>1e-9?Math.max(floorGain,Math.min(1,(mag-noise)/mag)):1;
          gains[k]=raw;
        }
        // Frequency smoothing reduces the watery artifacts of a hard spectral gate.
        const copy=gains.slice();for(let k=1;k<half;k++)gains[k]=(copy[k-1]+2*copy[k]+copy[k+1])/4;
        for(let k=0;k<=half;k++){const g=gains[k];re[k]*=g;im[k]*=g;if(k>0&&k<half){re[fftSize-k]*=g;im[fftSize-k]*=g;}}
        fft(re,im,true);
        for(let i=0;i<fftSize;i++){const ix=s+i;if(ix>=0&&ix<out.length){const w=windowFn[i];out[ix]+=re[i]*w;weight[ix]+=w*w;}}
        done++;if(done%32===0){onProgress(((ch+done/starts.length)/channelCount)*82,mode==='noise'?'Removing background noise…':'Enhancing audio…');await yieldUI();}
      }
      const clean=new Float32Array(buffer.length);
      for(let i=0;i<clean.length;i++)clean[i]=weight[i]>1e-6?out[i]/weight[i]:source[i];
      if(mode==='enhance'){
        const amount=strength;let peak=0;
        for(let i=0;i<clean.length;i++){let x=clean[i],a=Math.abs(x);if(a>.22){const compressed=.22+(a-.22)/(1+amount*1.8);x=Math.sign(x)*compressed;}clean[i]=x;peak=Math.max(peak,Math.abs(x));}
        const gain=Math.min(2.8,peak>1e-6?.90/peak:1);for(let i=0;i<clean.length;i++)clean[i]=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,clean[i]*gain));
      }
      channels.push(clean);onProgress(((ch+1)/channelCount)*86,mode==='noise'?'Finalizing cleaned audio…':'Finalizing enhanced audio…');await yieldUI();
    }
    return {numberOfChannels:channelCount,length:buffer.length,sampleRate:buffer.sampleRate,duration:buffer.duration,getChannelData:(ch)=>channels[ch]};
  }
  function audioBufferToWav(buffer){
    const channels=buffer.numberOfChannels,length=buffer.length,bytes=44+length*channels*2,ab=new ArrayBuffer(bytes),view=new DataView(ab);
    const write=(offset,string)=>{for(let i=0;i<string.length;i++)view.setUint8(offset+i,string.charCodeAt(i));};
    write(0,'RIFF');view.setUint32(4,bytes-8,true);write(8,'WAVE');write(12,'fmt ');view.setUint32(16,16,true);view.setUint16(20,1,true);view.setUint16(22,channels,true);view.setUint32(24,buffer.sampleRate,true);view.setUint32(28,buffer.sampleRate*channels*2,true);view.setUint16(32,channels*2,true);view.setUint16(34,16,true);write(36,'data');view.setUint32(40,bytes-44,true);
    const data=Array.from({length:channels},(_,ch)=>buffer.getChannelData(ch));let offset=44;
    for(let i=0;i<length;i++)for(let ch=0;ch<channels;ch++){const s=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,data[ch][i]));view.setInt16(offset,s<0?s*32768:s*32767,true);offset+=2;}
    return new Blob([ab],{type:'audio/wav'});
  }
  function resetAudioRuntime(clip){
    try{const el=audioOverlayEls&&audioOverlayEls[clip.id];if(el){el.pause();el.removeAttribute('src');el.load();}
      const nodes=audioOverlayNodes&&audioOverlayNodes[clip.id];if(nodes){Object.values(nodes).forEach(n=>{try{if(n&&typeof n.disconnect==='function')n.disconnect();}catch(_e){}});}
      if(audioOverlayEls)delete audioOverlayEls[clip.id];if(audioOverlayNodes)delete audioOverlayNodes[clip.id];
    }catch(err){console.warn('Audio runtime reset:',err);}
  }
  async function restoreProcessedAudioAssets(){
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    for(const clip of overlays){if(!clip||clip.type!=='audio'||!clip.audioProcessedAssetKey)continue;
      try{const blob=await readAudioAsset(clip.audioProcessedAssetKey);if(!blob)continue;resetAudioRuntime(clip);if(clip.__evcaV68ObjectUrl)URL.revokeObjectURL(clip.__evcaV68ObjectUrl);clip.__evcaV68ObjectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(blob);clip.src=clip.__evcaV68ObjectUrl;clip.sourceDuration=clip.sourceDuration||0;}
      catch(err){console.warn('Could not restore processed audio asset:',err);}
    }
    try{if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();}catch(_e){}
  }
  window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio=async function(mode){
    const clip=audioClip();if(!clip){showToast('Select an audio clip first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    if(!['enhance','noise'].includes(mode))return;
    if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying&&typeof pausePlayback==='function')pausePlayback();
    clip.audioProcessedModes=clip.audioProcessedModes&&typeof clip.audioProcessedModes==='object'?clip.audioProcessedModes:{};
    const showProgress=!clip.audioProcessedModes[mode];
    const update=showProgress?showAudioProgress(mode==='noise'?'Removing Background Noise':'Enhancing Audio'):function(){};
    const buttons=document.querySelectorAll('#evcaAudioPanel button');buttons.forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);
    try{
      update(3,'Loading source audio…');await yieldUI();
      // Each processing mode keeps the exact audio that existed before its first Apply.
      // Re-applying after a slider change uses that baseline instead of repeatedly filtering
      // an already-filtered WAV, preventing cumulative loss of voice quality.
      let bytes=null;
      const baseKey=clip.audioProcessBaseKeys&&clip.audioProcessBaseKeys[mode];
      if(clip.audioProcessedModes[mode]&&baseKey){
        try{const baseBlob=await readAudioAsset(baseKey);if(baseBlob)bytes=await baseBlob.arrayBuffer();}catch(_e){}
      }
      if(!bytes){
        const response=await fetch(clip.src);if(!response.ok)throw new Error('Could not read the selected audio source.');
        const sourceBlob=await response.blob();bytes=await sourceBlob.arrayBuffer();
        if(!baseKey){
          const newBaseKey='audio-'+String(clip.id)+'-'+mode+'-base-v70';
          try{await saveAudioAsset(newBaseKey,sourceBlob);clip.audioProcessBaseKeys=Object.assign({},clip.audioProcessBaseKeys||{},{[mode]:newBaseKey});}catch(storageErr){console.warn('Could not save the processing baseline; repeat processing will use the current clip audio.',storageErr);}
        }
      }
      update(8,'Decoding audio…');await yieldUI();
      const decodeCtx=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
      let sourceBuffer;try{sourceBuffer=await decodeCtx.decodeAudioData(bytes.slice(0));}finally{try{await decodeCtx.close();}catch(_e){}}
      const cfg=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings?window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(clip):{};
      const strength=(mode==='noise'?clamp(cfg.noiseStrength==null?70:cfg.noiseStrength,0,100):clamp(cfg.enhance==null?60:cfg.enhance,0,100))/100;
      const processed=await spectralClean(sourceBuffer,mode,strength,update);
      update(91,'Encoding processed audio…');await yieldUI();const blob=audioBufferToWav(processed);
      const assetKey='audio-'+String(clip.id)+'-v68';
      try{await saveAudioAsset(assetKey,blob);clip.audioProcessedAssetKey=assetKey;}catch(storageErr){console.warn('Audio persistence unavailable; keeping processed sound for this session.',storageErr);}
      resetAudioRuntime(clip);if(clip.__evcaV68ObjectUrl)URL.revokeObjectURL(clip.__evcaV68ObjectUrl);
      clip.__evcaV68ObjectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(blob);clip.src=clip.__evcaV68ObjectUrl;clip.sourceDuration=processed.duration;
      if(!clip.audioSettings)clip.audioSettings={};
      // The processing is baked into this WAV now, so turn off the matching live DSP stage
      // to avoid applying the same compression/gate twice during playback.
      // Keep the user's Enable switch on so they can immediately adjust the amount and
      // Apply again. The live preview/export graphs below detect audioProcessedModes and
      // skip the matching realtime stage because that processing is already baked into WAV.
      clip.audioProcessedMode=mode;
      clip.audioProcessedModes=Object.assign({},clip.audioProcessedModes||{},{[mode]:true});
      if(typeof generateAudioWaveform==='function'){
        try{const result=await generateAudioWaveform(clip.src);if(result&&result.peaks)clip.peaks=result.peaks;}catch(_e){}
      }
      if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();if(typeof syncTimelineInnerWidth==='function')syncTimelineInnerWidth();if(typeof renderRuler==='function')renderRuler();
      if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
      if(showProgress){
        update(100,'Applied',true);
        await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,1000));
        const progressBox=document.getElementById('evcaV68ProcessOverlay');
        if(progressBox)progressBox.classList.remove('active');
      }
      if(window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel)window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel(mode==='noise'?'audioNoise':'audioEnhance');
      autoPlayProcessedClip(clip);
    }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle offline audio processing failed:',err);if(showProgress){update(100,'Processing failed',true);setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('evcaV68ProcessOverlay')?.classList.remove('active'),1200);}showToast('Audio processing failed: '+(err?.message||'unsupported audio source'),{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:3000});}
    finally{buttons.forEach(b=>b.disabled=false);}
  };
  function autoPlayProcessedClip(clip){
    try{
      if(typeof pausePlayback==='function')pausePlayback();
      if(typeof setCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')setCurrentAbsoluteTime(Math.max(0,Number(clip.trimStart)||0));
      if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
      if(typeof startPlayback==='function')startPlayback();
      // startPlayback clears selections by design; restore this clip's contextual toolbar.
      selectedClip=clip.id;selectedKfIdx=null;
      document.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip.selected').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('selected'));
      const el=[...document.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]')].find(node=>String(node.getAttribute('data-overlay-id'))===String(clip.id));if(el)el.classList.add('selected');
      if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();
    }catch(err){console.warn('Could not auto-play processed audio:',err);}
  }
  const oldSetter=window.setEthvelleAudioSetting;
  if(typeof oldSetter==='function'&&!oldSetter.__evcaV68){
    // Enabling an effect is only a configuration change. Actual PCM processing is
    // deliberately started exclusively by the dedicated Apply button in the panel.
    const wrapped=function(property,value,commit){return oldSetter.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrapped.__evcaV68=true;window.setEthvelleAudioSetting=wrapped;
  }

  /* Audio panel buttons invoke actual PCM processing; visual controls remain live DSP. */
  const priorRenderer=window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel;
  if(typeof priorRenderer==='function'&&!priorRenderer.__evcaV68){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=priorRenderer.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='audioEnhance'||key==='audioNoise'){
        const host=$('evcaAudioPanel'),clip=audioClip();if(host){
          if(clip&&clip.audioProcessedMode===(key==='audioNoise'?'noise':'enhance')){const status=document.createElement('div');status.className='evca-v68-processed-status';status.textContent=key==='audioNoise'?'Background noise reduction applied to this clip':'Audio enhancement applied to this clip';host.appendChild(status);}
          const button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.className='evca-v68-process-button';button.textContent=key==='audioNoise'?'Remove Background Noise':'Enhance & Apply';button.onclick=()=>window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio(key==='audioNoise'?'noise':'enhance');host.appendChild(button);
        }
      }
      wireAudioSliderProgress($('evcaAudioPanel'));
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evcaV68=true;window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel=wrapped;
  }
  const priorOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof priorOpen==='function'&&!priorOpen.__evcaV68){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=priorOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(['audioEffects','audioVoice','audioAdjust','audioEnhance','audioNoise'].includes(key)){setTimeout(()=>{if(window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel)window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel(key);},0);}return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV68=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }

  /* Restore processed assets after opening a saved project. */
  const oldOpenEditor=window.openEditor;
  if(typeof oldOpenEditor==='function'&&!oldOpenEditor.__evcaV68){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldOpenEditor.apply(this,arguments);Promise.resolve(result).finally(()=>setTimeout(restoreProcessedAudioAssets,350));return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV68=true;window.openEditor=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',refreshAudioToolbar,{once:true});
  else{refreshAudioToolbar();}
})();

</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v69-zoom-audio-final-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));
  const selectedAudio=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&o.type==='audio'&&String(o.id)===String(selectedClip))||null:null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const isAnimationSelected=()=>selectedClip==='main'||(typeof selectedClip==='string'&&selectedClip.startsWith('seg'));

  /* Replace the bottom Custom tool with a zoom control alongside the transport keyframe button. */
  window.toggleCanvasZoomView=function(){
    const active=!!window.__ethvelleCustomAnimationMode;
    if(active){if(typeof window.closeCustomAnimationStudio==='function')window.closeCustomAnimationStudio();}
    else if(typeof window.openCustomAnimationStudio==='function')window.openCustomAnimationStudio();
    const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(btn){btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!active));btn.title=active?'Zoom canvas':'Exit canvas zoom';}
  };
  const oldOpenStudio=window.openCustomAnimationStudio;
  if(typeof oldOpenStudio==='function'&&!oldOpenStudio.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldOpenStudio.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      const hud=$('evcaCustomMotionHud');if(hud){const title=hud.querySelector('.evca-motion-title');if(title)title.textContent='Canvas Zoom';const exit=hud.querySelector('.evca-motion-top .evca-motion-btn');if(exit)exit.textContent='Normal Model';}
      const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(btn){btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');btn.title='Exit canvas zoom';}
    });return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.openCustomAnimationStudio=wrapped;
  }
  const oldCloseStudio=window.closeCustomAnimationStudio;
  if(typeof oldCloseStudio==='function'&&!oldCloseStudio.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldCloseStudio.apply(this,arguments);const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(btn){btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');btn.title='Zoom canvas';}return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.closeCustomAnimationStudio=wrapped;
  }
  function syncZoomButton(){
    const legacyCustomTool=$('clipCustomAnimTool');if(legacyCustomTool)legacyCustomTool.remove();
    const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(!btn)return;
    const show=isAnimationSelected();
    btn.style.display='inline-flex';
    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!!window.__ethvelleCustomAnimationMode));
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);syncZoomButton();return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);syncZoomButton();return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  /* Ensure transport button sync after entering/leaving the editor and after DOM construction. */
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',syncZoomButton,{once:true});else syncZoomButton();

  /* Final audio panel renderer: toggles are preferences only; expensive PCM processing starts
     exclusively when the user presses the dedicated Apply button. */
  let activeAudioPanel='';
  const audioDefs={
    audioEffects:{title:'Audio Effects',property:'effect',items:[['none','Original'],['echo','Echo'],['studio','Studio']]},
    audioVoice:{title:'Voice Filters',property:'voice',items:[['natural','Natural'],['clear','Clear'],['warm','Warm'],['deep','Deep'],['bright','Bright'],['radio','Radio'],['telephone','Telephone'],['soft','Soft']]}
  };
  function getCfg(clip){return window.getEthvelleAudioSettings?window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(clip):(clip.audioSettings=Object.assign({effect:'none',voice:'natural',pitch:0,pan:0,fadeIn:0,fadeOut:0,enhance:55,enhanceEnabled:false,noiseReduction:false,noiseStrength:65},clip.audioSettings||{}));}
  function choiceGrid(key,cfg){const d=audioDefs[key];return `<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">${d.title}</div><div class="evca-audio-choice-grid">${d.items.map(([v,label])=>`<button type="button" class="evca-audio-choice ${cfg[d.property]===v?'active':''}" onclick="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${d.property}','${v}',true)">${label}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;}
  function slider(id,label,value,min,max,step,unit,property){
    const formatted=unit==='st'?(Number(value)>0?'+':'')+Number(value).toFixed(1)+' st':unit==='s'?Number(value).toFixed(1)+' s':unit==='pan'?(Number(value)<0?'L '+Math.abs(Math.round(value))+'%':Number(value)>0?'R '+Math.round(value)+'%':'Center'):Math.round(value)+'%';
    return `<div class="evca-audio-slider-card"><div class="evca-audio-slider-head"><label for="${id}">${label}</label><output id="${id}Value">${formatted}</output></div><input class="evca-audio-slider" id="${id}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${value}" oninput="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${property}',this.value,false);document.getElementById('${id}Value').textContent=${unit==='st'?'(Number(this.value)>0?\'+\':\'\')+Number(this.value).toFixed(1)+\' st\'':unit==='s'?'Number(this.value).toFixed(1)+\' s\'':unit==='pan'?'(Number(this.value)<0?\'L \'+Math.abs(Math.round(Number(this.value)))+\'%\':Number(this.value)>0?\'R \'+Math.round(Number(this.value))+\'%\':\'Center\')':"Math.round(Number(this.value))+'%'"}" onchange="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${property}',this.value,true)"></div>`;
  }
  function switchField(label,property,checked){return `<label class="evca-small-switch"><span>${label}</span><input type="checkbox" ${checked?'checked':''} onchange="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${property}',this.checked,true)"><i></i></label>`;}
  function wirePanelRanges(host){
    if(!host)return;
    host.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]').forEach(input=>{
      const paint=()=>{const min=Number(input.min)||0,max=Number(input.max)||100,val=Number(input.value)||0;input.style.setProperty('--evca-progress',clamp(max>min?(val-min)/(max-min)*100:0,0,100)+'%');};
      if(!input.dataset.evcaV69Range){input.addEventListener('input',paint);input.dataset.evcaV69Range='1';}
      paint();
    });
  }
  window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel=function(key){
    const host=$('evcaAudioPanel'),clip=selectedAudio();if(!host)return;
    if(!clip){host.innerHTML='';return;}
    activeAudioPanel=key||activeAudioPanel;const cfg=getCfg(clip);
    if(audioDefs[key]){host.innerHTML=choiceGrid(key,cfg);return;}
    if(key==='audioAdjust'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">Audio Adjustment</div>${slider('evcaAudioPitchV69','Pitch',cfg.pitch,-12,12,.5,'st','pitch')}${slider('evcaAudioPanV69','Stereo Balance',cfg.pan,-100,100,1,'pan','pan')}${slider('evcaAudioFadeInV69','Fade In',cfg.fadeIn,0,8,.1,'s','fadeIn')}${slider('evcaAudioFadeOutV69','Fade Out',cfg.fadeOut,0,8,.1,'s','fadeOut')}</div>`;wirePanelRanges(host);return;
    }
    if(key==='audioEnhance'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel">${switchField('Enable enhancement','enhanceEnabled',!!cfg.enhanceEnabled)}${slider('evcaAudioEnhanceV69','Enhance Amount',cfg.enhance,0,100,1,'%','enhance')}<button type="button" class="evca-audio-apply-btn" ${cfg.enhanceEnabled?'':'disabled'} onclick="ethvelleApplyAudioProcess('enhance')">Apply Enhancement</button></div>`;wirePanelRanges(host);return;
    }
    if(key==='audioNoise'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel">${switchField('Remove background noise','noiseReduction',!!cfg.noiseReduction)}${slider('evcaAudioNoiseV69','Reduction Strength',cfg.noiseStrength,0,100,1,'%','noiseStrength')}<button type="button" class="evca-audio-apply-btn" ${cfg.noiseReduction?'':'disabled'} onclick="ethvelleApplyAudioProcess('noise')">Apply Noise Reduction</button></div>`;wirePanelRanges(host);return;
    }
  };
  function refreshLiveAudio(clip){
    try{
      const now=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
      const t=now>=Number(clip.trimStart)&&now<Number(clip.trimEnd)?now:Math.max(0,Number(clip.trimStart)||0)+.01;
      if(typeof window.renderAudioOverlayMedia==='function')window.renderAudioOverlayMedia(t);
    }catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle audio preview update:',e);}
  }
  function restartAudioOnce(clip){
    try{
      if(typeof window.pausePlayback==='function')window.pausePlayback();
      if(typeof window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime(Math.max(0,Number(clip.trimStart)||0));
      if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();
      if(typeof window.startPlayback==='function')window.startPlayback();
      // Re-arm selected clip UI after the player has started; this is one single restart call.
      selectedClip=clip.id;selectedKfIdx=null;
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{document.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip.selected').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('selected'));const node=[...document.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]')].find(el=>String(el.getAttribute('data-overlay-id'))===String(clip.id));if(node)node.classList.add('selected');if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();});
    }catch(e){console.warn('Audio autoplay:',e);}
  }
  window.setEthvelleAudioSetting=function(property,value,commit){
    const clip=selectedAudio();if(!clip)return;
    const cfg=getCfg(clip);
    if(property==='effect'&&['none','echo','studio'].includes(String(value)))cfg.effect=String(value);
    else if(property==='voice')cfg.voice=String(value);
    else if(property==='pitch')cfg.pitch=clamp(value,-12,12);
    else if(property==='pan')cfg.pan=clamp(value,-100,100);
    else if(property==='fadeIn'||property==='fadeOut')cfg[property]=clamp(value,0,8);
    else if(property==='enhance')cfg.enhance=clamp(value,0,100);
    else if(property==='enhanceEnabled')cfg.enhanceEnabled=!!value;
    else if(property==='noiseStrength')cfg.noiseStrength=clamp(value,0,100);
    else if(property==='noiseReduction')cfg.noiseReduction=!!value;
    refreshLiveAudio(clip);
    if(commit){try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState();}catch(e){console.warn('Audio setting save:',e);}if(activeAudioPanel)window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel(activeAudioPanel);
      // Selecting an effect/voice preset restarts playback exactly once from this clip's start.
      if(property==='effect'||property==='voice')restartAudioOnce(clip);
    }
  };
  const processBase=window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio;
  window.ethvelleApplyAudioProcess=async function(mode){
    const clip=selectedAudio();if(!clip||typeof processBase!=='function')return;
    const cfg=getCfg(clip);
    if(mode==='enhance'&&!cfg.enhanceEnabled){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Enable enhancement before applying',{duration:1500});return;}
    if(mode==='noise'&&!cfg.noiseReduction){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Enable noise reduction before applying',{duration:1500});return;}
    await window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio(mode);
  };
  if(typeof processBase==='function'&&!processBase.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=async function(mode){const clip=selectedAudio();const beforeMode=clip&&clip.audioProcessedMode;const beforeSrc=clip&&clip.src;const beforeAsset=clip&&clip.audioProcessedAssetKey;await processBase.apply(this,arguments);const applied=!!(clip&&clip.audioProcessedMode===mode&&(beforeMode!==mode||clip.src!==beforeSrc||clip.audioProcessedAssetKey!==beforeAsset));if(applied&&typeof window.closePanel==='function')window.closePanel();};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio=wrapped;
    window.ethvelleApplyAudioProcess=async function(mode){const clip=selectedAudio();if(!clip)return;const cfg=getCfg(clip);if(mode==='enhance'&&!cfg.enhanceEnabled){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Enable enhancement before applying',{duration:1500});return;}if(mode==='noise'&&!cfg.noiseReduction){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Enable noise reduction before applying',{duration:1500});return;}return window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio(mode);};
  }
  /* Give all audio panel openings the v69 renderer after the legacy panel shell is created. */
  const openBase=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof openBase==='function'&&!openBase.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=openBase.apply(this,arguments);if(['audioEffects','audioVoice','audioAdjust','audioEnhance','audioNoise'].includes(key)){requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel(key));}return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(e.target.closest('#evcaCustomMotionHud .evca-motion-top .evca-motion-btn')){
      setTimeout(()=>{const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(btn){btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');btn.title='Zoom canvas';}},0);
    }
  },true);
})();
</script>
